+The GitHub Actions APK workflow signs release builds with a dedicated keystore
+when these repository secrets are configured: `ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64` (the
+base64-encoded keystore), `ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD`, `ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS`, and
+`ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD`. Never commit the keystore or its passwords. Without
+these secrets, the workflow uses Expo's default debug signing; do not distribute
+that APK. Switching from an existing signing key requires users to reinstall
+the app once.

+  name: string;
+  price: number;
+}
+
+interface ProductReview {

+  reviewCount: number;
+}
+

+  price: string | null;
+  sale_price?: string | null;
+  brand?: string | null;

+interface ProductFetchError {
+  error: string;
+  searchedSku?: string;
+  foundSku?: string;
+  challenge?: ChallengeData;
+  results?: TextSearchResult[];
+}
+
+interface ChallengeData {
+  url?: string;
+  [key: string]: unknown;
+}
+

+  status: 'solved' | 'failed';
+  finalUrl?: string;
+  userAgent?: string | null;
+  reason?: string;
+  productData?: ProductData;
+}

+  url: string;

+  stockText: string | null;
+}
+

+  singleUrl?: string;
+  results?: TextSearchResult[];
+  error?: string;
+  challenge?: ChallengeData;
+}
+
+interface SearchHistoryEntry {
+  sku: string;
+  name: string;
+  price: string | null;
+  date: string;
+}
+
+type FetchProductResult = ProductData | ProductFetchError;
+type StoredListItem = Omit<ListItem, 'quantity'> & { quantity?: number };
+type StoredItemList = Omit<ItemList, 'items'> & { items: StoredListItem[] };
+
+type PanGestureEvent = {
+  nativeEvent: { translationX: number; translationY: number; state?: number };
+};
+type PinchGestureEvent = { nativeEvent: { scale: number; state?: number } };
+
+function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
+  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null;
+}
+
+function parseStoredArray<T>(
+  raw: string | null,
+  isItem: (value: unknown) => value is T
+): T[] {
+  const parsed: unknown = safeParse(raw);
+  return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter(isItem) : [];
+}
+
+function isHistoryEntry(value: unknown): value is SearchHistoryEntry {
+  return isRecord(value)
+    && typeof value.sku === 'string'
+    && typeof value.name === 'string'
+    && (typeof value.price === 'string' || value.price === null)
+    && typeof value.date === 'string';
+}
+
+function isProductData(value: unknown): value is ProductData {
+  if (!isRecord(value)) return false;
+  return typeof value.sku === 'string'
+    && typeof value.name === 'string'
+    && (typeof value.price === 'string' || value.price === null);
+}
+
+function isStoredListItem(value: unknown): value is StoredListItem {
+  return isRecord(value)
+    && typeof value.id === 'string'
+    && typeof value.sku === 'string'
+    && typeof value.name === 'string'
+    && (typeof value.price === 'number' || value.price === null)
+    && typeof value.rawPrice === 'string'
+    && typeof value.storeId === 'string'
+    && typeof value.date === 'string'
+    && (value.quantity === undefined || typeof value.quantity === 'number')
+    && (value.stockText === undefined || typeof value.stockText === 'string' || value.stockText === null)
+    && (value.inStock === undefined || typeof value.inStock === 'boolean' || value.inStock === null);
+}
+
+function isStoredList(value: unknown): value is StoredItemList {
+  if (!isRecord(value) || typeof value.id !== 'string' || typeof value.name !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(value.items)) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  return value.items.every(isStoredListItem);
+}
+
+function parseStoredLists(raw: string | null): ItemList[] {
+  return parseStoredArray(raw, isStoredList).map((list) => ({
+    ...list,
+    items: list.items.map((item) => ({ ...item, quantity: item.quantity ?? 1 })),
+  }));
+}
+
+function errorMessage(error: unknown, fallback: string): string {
+  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : fallback;
+}

+interface ChallengeResult {

+interface ChallengeMessage {
+  isChallenge: true;
+  url: string;

+
+const isFiniteNumber = (value: unknown): value is number =>
+  typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
+
+const isChallengeResult = (value: unknown): value is ChallengeResult =>
+  typeof value === 'object' && value !== null &&
+  (!('status' in value) ||
+    value.status === 'solved' ||
+    value.status === 'cancelled' ||
+    value.status === 'error') &&
+  (!('userAgent' in value) || typeof value.userAgent === 'string') &&
+  ('status' in value || 'userAgent' in value);
+
+const isChallengeMessage = (value: unknown): value is ChallengeMessage =>
+  typeof value === 'object' && value !== null &&
+  'isChallenge' in value && value.isChallenge === true &&
+  'url' in value && typeof value.url === 'string';

diff --git a/MicroScraper/scripts/configureAndroidSigning.mjs b/MicroScraper/scripts/configureAndroidSigning.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c330601
--- /dev/null
+++ b/MicroScraper/scripts/configureAndroidSigning.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,67 @@
+// Run after `expo prebuild --platform android` in CI.
+//
+// Expo's generated build.gradle signs release builds with a debug keystore that
+// is identical for every Expo project, so anyone could produce an APK that
+// installs over this app as an "update". This script points the release build at
+// a real keystore instead, read from environment variables:
+//
+//   ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH      path to the .jks/.keystore file
+//   ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD  keystore password
+//   ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS          key alias
+//   ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD       key password
+//
+// If any are missing it changes nothing and exits 0, so unsigned/debug-signed
+// builds keep working. It is idempotent.
+import fs from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const gradlePath = path.resolve('android/app/build.gradle');
+const required = ['ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH', 'ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD', 'ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS', 'ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD'];
+const missing = required.filter((name) => !process.env[name]);
+
+if (missing.length > 0) {
+  console.warn(`[signing] Missing ${missing.join(', ')}. Leaving the default debug signing in place.`);
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+
+if (!fs.existsSync(gradlePath)) {
+  console.error(`[signing] ${gradlePath} not found. Run expo prebuild first.`);
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+if (!fs.existsSync(process.env.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH)) {
+  console.error(`[signing] Keystore not found at ${process.env.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH}.`);
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+let gradle = fs.readFileSync(gradlePath, 'utf8');
+if (gradle.includes('MICRO_SKU_RELEASE_SIGNING')) {
+  console.log('[signing] Already configured.');
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+
+const releaseConfig = `        release { // MICRO_SKU_RELEASE_SIGNING
+            storeFile file(System.getenv('ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH'))
+            storePassword System.getenv('ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD')
+            keyAlias System.getenv('ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS')
+            keyPassword System.getenv('ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD')
+        }
+`;
+
+// 1. Add a release signingConfig right after the existing signingConfigs { debug {...} } block.
+const signingBlock = /(signingConfigs\s*\{\s*debug\s*\{[\s\S]*?\n\s{8}\}\n)/;
+if (!signingBlock.test(gradle)) {
+  console.error('[signing] Could not find the signingConfigs block in build.gradle.');
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+gradle = gradle.replace(signingBlock, `$1${releaseConfig}`);
+
+// 2. Point the release buildType at it.
+const releaseBuildType = /(buildTypes\s*\{[\s\S]*?release\s*\{[\s\S]*?)signingConfig signingConfigs\.debug/;
+if (!releaseBuildType.test(gradle)) {
+  console.error('[signing] Could not find the release buildType signingConfig.');
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+gradle = gradle.replace(releaseBuildType, '$1signingConfig signingConfigs.release');
+
+fs.writeFileSync(gradlePath, gradle);
+console.log('[signing] Release builds will use the configured keystore.');
diff --git a/MicroScraper/scripts/configureAndroidSigning.test.mjs b/MicroScraper/scripts/configureAndroidSigning.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..63956a4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/MicroScraper/scripts/configureAndroidSigning.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,75 @@
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import test from 'node:test';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const scriptPath = fileURLToPath(new URL('./configureAndroidSigning.mjs', import.meta.url));
+const requiredSecrets = [
+  'ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH',
+  'ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD',
+  'ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS',
+  'ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD',
+];
+
+const runSigningScript = (cwd, env) =>
+  spawnSync(process.execPath, [scriptPath], { cwd, env, encoding: 'utf8' });
+
+test('leaves generated signing unchanged when release secrets are absent', async (t) => {
+  const cwd = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'micro-sku-signing-'));
+  t.after(() => rm(cwd, { recursive: true, force: true }));
+
+  const env = { ...process.env };
+  for (const secret of requiredSecrets) delete env[secret];
+
+  const result = runSigningScript(cwd, env);
+
+  assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr);
+  assert.match(result.stderr, /Leaving the default debug signing in place/);
+});
+
+test('configures release signing once and is idempotent', async (t) => {
+  const cwd = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'micro-sku-signing-'));
+  t.after(() => rm(cwd, { recursive: true, force: true }));
+
+  const gradlePath = path.join(cwd, 'android', 'app', 'build.gradle');
+  const keystorePath = path.join(cwd, 'release.keystore');
+  await mkdir(path.dirname(gradlePath), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(keystorePath, 'test-keystore');
+  await writeFile(gradlePath, `android {
+    signingConfigs {
+        debug {
+            storeFile file('debug.keystore')
+        }
+    }
+    buildTypes {
+        debug {
+            signingConfig signingConfigs.debug
+        }
+        release {
+            signingConfig signingConfigs.debug
+        }
+    }
+}
+`);
+
+  const env = {
+    ...process.env,
+    ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PATH: keystorePath,
+    ANDROID_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD: 'store-password',
+    ANDROID_KEY_ALIAS: 'release',
+    ANDROID_KEY_PASSWORD: 'key-password',
+  };
+  const firstRun = runSigningScript(cwd, env);
+  assert.equal(firstRun.status, 0, firstRun.stderr);
+
+  const configuredGradle = await readFile(gradlePath, 'utf8');
+  assert.equal((configuredGradle.match(/MICRO_SKU_RELEASE_SIGNING/g) || []).length, 1);
+  assert.match(configuredGradle, /signingConfig signingConfigs\.release/);
+
+  const secondRun = runSigningScript(cwd, env);
+  assert.equal(secondRun.status, 0, secondRun.stderr);
+  assert.equal(await readFile(gradlePath, 'utf8'), configuredGradle);
+});

diff --git a/MicroScraper/services/databaseSchema.mjs b/MicroScraper/services/databaseSchema.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..dfdb9a6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/MicroScraper/services/databaseSchema.mjs

+export async function createDatabaseTables(db) {
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_categories (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
+      display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
+      order_index INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      required INTEGER DEFAULT 0
+    );
+  `);
+
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS components (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      sku TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
+      name TEXT NOT NULL,
+      category_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      brand TEXT,
+      price REAL,
+      sale_price REAL,
+      image_url TEXT,
+      url TEXT,
+      specs TEXT,
+      created_at INTEGER DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
+      FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES component_categories(id)
+    );
+  `);
+
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_specs (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      component_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      spec_key TEXT NOT NULL,
+      spec_value TEXT NOT NULL,
+      FOREIGN KEY (component_id) REFERENCES components(id),
+      UNIQUE(component_id, spec_key)
+    );
+  `);
+
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS compatibility_rules (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      rule_type TEXT NOT NULL,
+      component1_category TEXT NOT NULL,
+      component1_spec_key TEXT NOT NULL,
+      component1_spec_value TEXT NOT NULL,
+      component2_category TEXT NOT NULL,
+      component2_spec_key TEXT NOT NULL,
+      component2_spec_value TEXT NOT NULL,
+      compatible INTEGER DEFAULT 1
+    );
+  `);
+
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bundles (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      name TEXT NOT NULL,
+      description TEXT,
+      total_price REAL,
+      bundle_price REAL,
+      savings REAL,
+      url TEXT,
+      created_at INTEGER DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
+    );
+  `);
+
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bundle_components (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      bundle_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      component_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      FOREIGN KEY (bundle_id) REFERENCES bundles(id),
+      FOREIGN KEY (component_id) REFERENCES components(id),
+      UNIQUE(bundle_id, component_id)
+    );
+  `);
+
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_builds (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      name TEXT NOT NULL,
+      notes TEXT,
+      total_price REAL,
+      created_at INTEGER DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
+      updated_at INTEGER DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
+    );
+  `);
+
+  await db.execAsync(`
+    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS build_components (
+      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+      build_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      component_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      category_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+      quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
+      FOREIGN KEY (build_id) REFERENCES user_builds(id),
+      FOREIGN KEY (component_id) REFERENCES components(id),
+      FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES component_categories(id),
+      UNIQUE(build_id, category_id, component_id)
+    );
+  `);
+}
+
+export async function initializeCategories(db) {
+  const categories = [
+    { name: 'cpu', display_name: 'CPU (Processor)', order_index: 1, required: 1 },
+    { name: 'motherboard', display_name: 'Motherboard', order_index: 2, required: 1 },
+    { name: 'ram', display_name: 'Memory (RAM)', order_index: 3, required: 1 },
+    { name: 'gpu', display_name: 'Graphics Card', order_index: 4, required: 0 },
+    { name: 'storage', display_name: 'Storage', order_index: 5, required: 1 },
+    { name: 'psu', display_name: 'Power Supply', order_index: 6, required: 1 },
+    { name: 'case', display_name: 'Case', order_index: 7, required: 1 },
+    { name: 'cooler', display_name: 'CPU Cooler', order_index: 8, required: 0 },
+    { name: 'case_fans', display_name: 'Case Fans', order_index: 9, required: 0 },
+    { name: 'os', display_name: 'Operating System', order_index: 10, required: 0 },
+  ];
+
+  for (const category of categories) {
+    await db.runAsync(
+      `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO component_categories (name, display_name, order_index, required)
+       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)`,
+      [category.name, category.display_name, category.order_index, category.required]
+    );
+  }
+}
+
+export async function initializeCompatibilityRules(db) {
+  const rules = [
+    {
+      rule_type: 'socket_match',
+      component1_category: 'cpu',
+      component1_spec_key: 'socket',
+      component1_spec_value: 'LGA1700',
+      component2_category: 'motherboard',
+      component2_spec_key: 'socket',
+      component2_spec_value: 'LGA1700',
+      compatible: 1,
+    },
+    {
+      rule_type: 'socket_match',
+      component1_category: 'cpu',
+      component1_spec_key: 'socket',
+      component1_spec_value: 'AM5',
+      component2_category: 'motherboard',
+      component2_spec_key: 'socket',
+      component2_spec_value: 'AM5',
+      compatible: 1,
+    },
+    {
+      rule_type: 'ram_type',
+      component1_category: 'motherboard',
+      component1_spec_key: 'memory_type',
+      component1_spec_value: 'DDR5',
+      component2_category: 'ram',
+      component2_spec_key: 'type',
+      component2_spec_value: 'DDR5',
+      compatible: 1,
+    },
+    {
+      rule_type: 'ram_type',
+      component1_category: 'motherboard',
+      component1_spec_key: 'memory_type',
+      component1_spec_value: 'DDR4',
+      component2_category: 'ram',
+      component2_spec_key: 'type',
+      component2_spec_value: 'DDR4',
+      compatible: 1,
+    },
+    {
+      rule_type: 'form_factor',
+      component1_category: 'motherboard',
+      component1_spec_key: 'form_factor',
+      component1_spec_value: 'ATX',
+      component2_category: 'case',
+      component2_spec_key: 'supported_form_factors',
+      component2_spec_value: 'ATX',
+      compatible: 1,
+    },
+  ];
+
+  for (const rule of rules) {
+    await db.runAsync(
+      `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO compatibility_rules
+       (rule_type, component1_category, component1_spec_key, component1_spec_value,
+        component2_category, component2_spec_key, component2_spec_value, compatible)
+       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
+      [
+        rule.rule_type,
+        rule.component1_category,
+        rule.component1_spec_key,
+        rule.component1_spec_value,
+        rule.component2_category,
+        rule.component2_spec_key,
+        rule.component2_spec_value,
+        rule.compatible,
+      ]
+    );
+  }
+}
diff --git a/MicroScraper/services/databaseSchema.test.mjs b/MicroScraper/services/databaseSchema.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e78c2e5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/MicroScraper/services/databaseSchema.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,48 @@
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import test from 'node:test';
+import {
+  createDatabaseTables,
+  initializeCategories,
+  initializeCompatibilityRules,
+} from './databaseSchema.mjs';
+
+test('initializes PC Builder tables and seed data through the provided database handle', async () => {
+  const schemaStatements = [];
+  const insertStatements = [];
+  const db = {
+    async execAsync(statement) {
+      schemaStatements.push(statement);
+    },
+    async runAsync(statement, values) {
+      insertStatements.push({ statement, values });
+    },
+  };
+
+  await createDatabaseTables(db);
+  await initializeCategories(db);
+  await initializeCompatibilityRules(db);
+
+  assert.equal(schemaStatements.length, 8);
+  for (const table of [
+    'component_categories',
+    'components',
+    'component_specs',
+    'compatibility_rules',
+    'bundles',
+    'bundle_components',
+    'user_builds',
+    'build_components',
+  ]) {
+    assert.ok(schemaStatements.some(statement => statement.includes(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ${table}`)));
+  }
+
+  const categoryInserts = insertStatements.filter(({ statement }) =>
+    statement.includes('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO component_categories')
+  );
+  const compatibilityInserts = insertStatements.filter(({ statement }) =>
+    statement.includes('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO compatibility_rules')
+  );
+  assert.equal(categoryInserts.length, 10);
+  assert.equal(compatibilityInserts.length, 5);
+  assert.equal(insertStatements.length, 15);
+});

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b04b838
--- /dev/null
+++ b/MicroScraper/services/userAgent.js
@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+// User agent for the Android challenge WebView.
+//
+// The stock Android WebView UA contains "; wv)" and "Version/4.0" tokens that
+// mark it as an embedded WebView rather than Chrome. iOS already overrides its
+// WebView UA to look like Safari (see applicationNameForUserAgent in the
+// WebView components), and scraper.ts sends a Chrome UA for native requests.
+// This gives Android the same treatment, using Chrome's "reduced" UA format.
+//
+// Keep the Chrome major version reasonably close to the installed Android
+// System WebView, and bump it every few months. The solved WebView reports this
+// string via navigator.userAgent, which setScraperUserAgent() then reuses for
+// native requests, so the whole session stays consistent.
+export const ANDROID_WEBVIEW_USER_AGENT =
+  'Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 10; K) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/140.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36';

-                        <h2 id="support-heading" class="accordion__heading">
-                            <button class="accordion__button" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#collapse-support" aria-expanded="true" aria-controls="collapse-support">
-                                Warranty & Support
-                            </button>
-                        </h2>
-                        <div class="collapse show" id="collapse-support" aria-labelledby="support-heading">
-                            <div class="accordion__body">
-
-                                <div id="warranty">
-<h3>Vendor Warranty:</h3>
-<ul>
-<li class="warranty-item"><div>Parts: 1 Year Limited</div></li><li class="warranty-item"><div>Labor: 1 Year Limited</div></li></ul>
-</div>
-<div id="returns">
-<div class="returns"><h3>Micro Center 15 Day Return Policy</h3><p>We guarantee your satisfaction on every product we sell with a full refund - and you won't even need a receipt.* We want you to be satisfied with your Micro Center purchase. However, if you need help or need to return an item, we're here for you!</p>
-<p>If an item you have purchased from us is not working as expected, please visit one of our in-store Knowledge Experts for free help, where they can solve your problem or even exchange the item for a product that better suits your needs.</p>
-<p>If you need to return an item, simply bring it back to any Micro Center store for a full refund or exchange.</p>
-<p>*If you are a Micro Center Insider or if you have provided us with validated contact information (name, address, email address), you won't even need your receipt.</p>
-<p><strong><a href="https://community.microcenter.com/discussion/12693/accelerate-your-returns-with-micro-centers-online-return-check-in/p1">Accelerate Your Returns with Micro Center's Online Return Check-in!</a></strong>
-</p>
-<p>Desktop / Notebook Computers, Tablets, Processors, Motherboards, Digital Cameras, Projectors, Cell Phones, 3D Printers, 3D Scanners, drones, and specific Branded Game Simulator Accessories may be returned within 15 days of purchase. All other products may be returned within 30 days of purchase. Merchandise must be in new condition, with original carton/UPC, and all packaging/accessories/materials. If you need to return software, a game or a movie, remember that you may not retain any copies - it's not legal, and it's not nice. </p>
-<p>VR headsets and Headphones (including AirPods, earbuds, and over-the-ear products) are only eligible for return if deemed defective.</p>
-<p><strong>Aerial Drones</strong><br />
-Micro Center is happy to honor its customary 15-day return policy for Aerial Drone returns due to product defect or customer dissatisfaction.
-Unfortunately, we cannot accept returns with damage due to pilot error. So let&rsquo;s be careful out there!</p>
-<p>As of December 21, 2015 all drones, regardless of purchase
-date, between 0.55 lb (8.82 oz, 250 g, 0.25 kg) and 55 lb take-off weight are required by
-the FAA to be registered for outdoor use. Details regarding registration can be
-found at: <a href="https://www.faa.gov/uas/getting_started/">https://www.faa.gov/uas/getting_started/</a></p>
-<p>&nbsp;</p>
-<br />
-Refunds will be credited to the credit card or debit card account used for the original purchase.
-<p>&nbsp;</p></div></div>

-<h3>Support for Micro Center purchases</h3>
-<p>If you need help with products purchased from Micro Center, please contact one of our knowledgeable tech support reps using the online chat button below, or <a href="/site/content/tech-support.aspx" target="_blank" onclick="ga('mc.send','event','tsup','warsuplib','wsl_techsup');">visit our Tech Support page</a> for additional options and helpful information.</p>

For new PowerSpec computers, this free assistance is extended to one year from the date of purchase. Your invoice reference number will be required for free assistance.</p>
